- `hmh07-lua-follow4:026` (original lines 2105-2115): Builds an FFA result bar with emblem, sanitized name and points model links. NoPlayers, Hidden and IsLocalPlayer are stored predicates; team-count/row subscriptions request reevaluation. It displays a result and does not award or persist it. Creates self via LUI.UIElement.new, SceneBlur via LUI.UIImage.new, DarkBacking via LUI.UIImage.new, NoiseTiledBacking via LUI.UIImage.new, Frame via CoD.StartMenuOptionsMainFrame.new, EmblemBacking via LUI.UIImage.new, EmblemBacking3 via LUI.UIImage.new, EmblemBacking2 via LUI.UIImage.new, GlowTexture via LUI.UIImage.new, Glow via LUI.UIImage.new, Emblem via LUI.UIImage.new, Name via LUI.UIText.new, PlaceBanner via LUI.UIImage.new, GlowSphere via LUI.UIImage.new, GlowRing via LUI.UIImage.new, PlaceText via LUI.UIText.new, BGDotPatternLarge via LUI.UIImage.new, Score via LUI.UIText.new, TiledShaderImage1 via LUI.UIImage.new, TiledShaderImage2 via LUI.UIImage.new, TiledShaderImage3 via LUI.UIImage.new, UnderLine1 via LUI.UIImage.new, UnderLine2 via LUI.UIImage.new, UnderLine3 via LUI.UIImage.new. Stores state predicates NoPlayers, Hidden, IsLocalPlayer; their body effects require state-system evaluation.

- `hmh07-lua:041` (original lines 2105-2115): Builds the WZ back HUD children including team/player/ammo/inventory/compass/collapse/reinsertion/death notifications. Spawn selection hides deployable notifications; dead+not hacked+not spawn-select gates DeathCamContainer. KilledBy is locally always hidden; killer identity is hidden outside killcam, binds Attacker and updates emblem/name/rank views only for nonnil data. Overall caster-profile/spawn-select hides are separate predicates. The constructor wires child source contracts; it does not reconstruct every child, event producer or server state.

- `hmh07-lua:186` (original lines 2105-2105, 2105-2115): Hud_WZ directly constructs this result widget. SetupGameEndScoreWidget and DisableAutoButtonCallback run first; demo.processedKeyframeJump and lobbyRoot.publicLobby.stage are initialized to0 (real model writes, not backend stage proof). Builds result/outcome/eliminator/status/timer/deathcam/VOIP/quit/placement/killer/weapon/AAR/director/crosshair children. The constructor requests a GameScore/titleString subscription. Only if its callback is delivered and model:get() is nonnil, it requests LocalizeToUpperString with that value and passes the unknown result to Outcome:setText, if earlier accesses/calls succeed; nil takes no explicit setText arm. Callback delivery, localization/case result and displayed effect remain unresolved; aliveTeammateCount drives DownNotOut art; GameScore model forwarded to eliminator/challenge/killer/weapon displays. Victory controls challenge coin visibility. meansOfDeath>0 hides killer/weapon displays and inversely reveals self-caused/means-of-death displays. Attacker identity model rebinding first removes and nils old per-field subscription, dereferences new attackerClientModel, then subscribes xuid/clientNum/rank/rankIcon; analogous clientModel rebinding populates MistakesWereMade identity. Calling card receives full client model; nonnil callbacks update emblem/name/cleaned clan/rank/icon. No nil update clears old displayed text explicitly. VOIP HudStart has a recovered early-return/branch shape (exact trace) and ShowForCodCaster AlwaysFalse; do not replace it with an assumed visibility formula. Outcome state family: ShowOutcomeTransition with no killcam -> Victory/PC; defeat/team_elimination with no killcam -> Defeat/GameOver variants; elimination -> Elimination. Platoon variants additionally platoonCount!=0; PC variants additionally IsPC. Non-PC named states do not themselves contain not-IsPC, so do not assume exclusivity; LUI arbitration stays external. State/current/visibility/profile/lobby/teammate/AAR/input subscriptions reevaluate state/buttons. Square prioritizes console spectate (hide AAR/send skip_deathcam/clear transition/location/hide occluder), then console online AAR close under ui_showWZAAR/noncustom/not-spectate. Triangle redeploy requires private host, all members can redeploy, no matchmaking, redeploy_enabled and noncustom. Cross console deathcam requires eligibility, non-PC, not alternate killcam button, not UI-active/demo/default and viewed report; sends play_deathcam, sets hasPlayedDeathCam, plays alias/music. Circle console hold-finished quits; PC branches quit on non-held conditions; contextual2 handles PC exit. Each action returns true when taken, label callbacks often return false despite valid action, exact source retained. setState CallOriginalFirst hook branches demo/console/PC-occluded focus/intro/cursor/chat behavior; keyframeJump clears transition only during demo. Close is registered via CallOriginalSecond to an unselected __onClose; optional postload then focus/safe-area setup and return. Assets, backend result/rank/AAR provenance, hook order/cleanup outside scope, native menu response and actual execution remain open.

KillcamArena predicate retains empty/missing-return arms and later IsArenaMode assignment literally; Killcam predicate combines in-killcam/nemesis bits. Two clientNum callbacks request Attacker/Victim datasource refresh. Installs class close and optional PostLoadFunc, returns self; no killcam gameplay outcome or repaired predicate is inferred.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:113` (original lines 2274-2274, 2274-2274, 2274-2274, 2269-2278): GetClientList annotates controlling-session clients using private-party membership (special ZM-private uses same session), nested xuid/simpleLobbyID scans, sorts by LobbyMemberSort, then marks adjacent-party flags and returns mutated sessionClients. NavigateToLobby returns for same target or trial/age/limited/access warnings; otherwise records navigation action and saved history before local/max-client warnings, sets optional training black screen and emits OnGoForward. LobbyGoBack clears saved state then prioritizes selection-screen refresh, manual timer stop, ZM ready-down/offline overlay, WZ public party-leave event, MP host party back, training outro callback and generic leave popup/with-party policy. StartSearch emits async start only when both local blockers false and playlist preparation succeeds. Vote select plays sound before native vote; location forwards private-lobby native update. AllMembersCanRedeploy returns false only on a nonnil location outside three allowed enums, true otherwise including empty/missing per-client locations. Unknown enums, data/native outcomes and original HMH binding stay explicit.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:077` (original lines 2105-2115, 2105-2115): Builds killer card presentation and binds entire element model to calling card. Nonnil Attacker xuid/clientNum/rank/rankIcon callbacks update emblem, name, cleaned clan, max-rank-localized rank text and icon. Nil updates do not clear old fields. Hidden predicate AlwaysFalse; CustomGames delegates IsWZCustomGame. Registers close, optional postload, returns self. It displays supplied identity without establishing who killed whom, rank truth or backend provenance.
